##### Raw second transcription

```yaml
lines:
  - id: household.h_status_hoh
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Filing status is head of household"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "hoh"}]}
  - id: household.h_status_married
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Filing status is married filing jointly, married filing separately or qualifying surviving spouse"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfs"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "qss"}]}
  - id: household.marriage_possible
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Marital status questions apply: you file with a married status (married filing jointly, married filing separately or qualifying surviving spouse), or you have ever been married"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [household.h_status_married, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [household.ever_married, {const: true}]}
    note: "The IRS pages do not name this result. It is read as the gate for the marital-status facts. A person who files with a married status, or who has ever been married, may be married or considered married on December 31, 2025 (Pub. 501, Marital Status). A person who was never married is unmarried (i1040gi, Single)."
  - id: household.h_test1_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of dependents who meet Test 1 (your dependent parent whose main home for all of 2025 you paid over half the cost of keeping up)"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: dependent
      field: dependent.one
      where: {op: test, eq: [dependent.hoh_test1, {const: true}]}
  - id: household.h_test2_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of persons who meet Test 2 (lived in your home for more than half of 2025 as a qualifying person)"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: dependent
      field: dependent.one
      where: {op: test, eq: [dependent.hoh_test2, {const: true}]}
  - id: household.h_has_test1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Test 1 applies"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [household.h_test1_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]}
  - id: household.h_has_test2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "A Test 2 qualifying person lived with you"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [household.h_test2_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]}
  - id: household.h_hoh_qualifies
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Either Test 1 applies, or Test 2 applies (you paid over half the cost of keeping up a home in which you and a qualifying person lived)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [household.h_has_test1, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [household.h_has_test2, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [household.home_cost, {const: true}]}
    note: "Test 1 has its own cost-of-home condition, for the parent's home, which is read as included in dependent.hoh_test1. Test 2 requires that you paid over half the cost of keeping up the home you lived in, which is household.home_cost."
  - id: household.h_married_not_unmarried
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Married at the end of 2025 and not considered unmarried"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [household.married_dec31, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [household.considered_unmarried, {const: false}]}
  - id: household.hoh_fails
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Head of household is claimed, but you are not unmarried (or considered unmarried) or neither Test 1 nor Test 2 applies"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [household.h_status_hoh, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [household.h_married_not_unmarried, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [household.spouse_died_this_year, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, not: {op: test, eq: [household.h_hoh_qualifies, {const: true}]}}
    note: "The IRS pages do not name this result. It is read as the check of the head of household box against i1040gi p. 14: you check the box only if you are unmarried (or considered unmarried) and either Test 1 or Test 2 applies. If your spouse died in 2025, you are considered married for the whole year (Pub. 501), so that case also fails. A failure of the cost-of-home condition is covered by household.h_hoh_qualifies."
```


Second transcription's note: The IRS pages do not name this result. It is read as the check of the head of household box against i1040gi p. 14: you check the box only if you are unmarried (or considered unmarried) and either Test 1 or Test 2 applies. If your spouse died in 2025, you are considered married for the whole year (Pub. 501), so that case also fails. A failure of the cost-of-home condition is covered by household.h_hoh_qualifies.

Ruling: open
Reviewed in: pass de16cc0a21be
